# Datapack validation

Generated by `chaoslab datapack` with no network access. This checks the
custom mod's configuration contract before any Java exists: every JSON file
under `mod/datapack/`, `mod/examples/` and `mod/config/` is validated against
the JSON Schemas in `mod/schema/`, plus the cross-file rules a schema cannot
express. `mod/datapack/` is what the mod ships; `mod/examples/` is the
validation fixture set and is not shipped.

Result: PASS — 4454 file(s) validated against 8 schema(s); 0 error(s).

## Schemas

| schema | title | registry |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `advancement.schema.json` | Minecraft advancement | advancement |
| `curse.schema.json` | Gacha curse | curse |
| `entry.schema.json` | Gacha entry | entry |
| `feat.schema.json` | Gacha feat | feat |
| `loot_modifier.schema.json` | Gacha loot modifier | loot_modifiers |
| `module.schema.json` | Gacha machine module | module |
| `power_score.schema.json` | Power Score config | config |
| `recipe.schema.json` | Gacha recipe | recipes |

### Module definitions

- `gacha:downgrade_reroll`: downgrade_reroll, bands 1
- `gacha:gamble`: gamble, stakes 2, 4, 6, 10, 12, 16; outcome table below.

| roll | outcome |
| --- | --- |
| 1 | destroyed |
| 2–7 | tier_down |
| 8–12 | nothing |
| 13–16 | new_kind |
| 17–19 | twin_core |
| 20 | tier_up |

### Power score default

`mod/config/power_score.json` (the answered D3 default; the decision sheet is the
source of record).

| input | weight |
| --- | --- |
| entries | 0.36 |
| gear | 0.36 |
| stage | 0.28 |

- Range: ±0.10
- Weight bounds: 0.05 .. 0.80
- Score clamp: 0.0 .. 1.0
- Reference: gear 538.4, entries 667

## Fixtures (mod/examples, not shipped)

Files: 22.

- by registry: entry 11, curse 5, feat 4, loot_modifiers 2
- entries by kind: familiar 4, item 2, trait 2, ability 1, modifier 1, system 1
- adapters: gacha:mob 3, gacha:effect 2, gacha:item 2, gacha:attribute 1, gacha:command 1
- hook verbs: grant_ticket 2, modify_ticket 2, refund 2, reroll_ticket 2, attach_modifier 1, gamble_tickets 1, unlock_machine_action 1
- curses by hook: attribute 2, event 1, inventory 1, mob 1
- feats by trigger: stat 2, advancement 1, visit 1

## Checks beyond JSON Schema

- registry directories map to schemas; unknown paths are errors
- ids derive from file paths and are unique across the whole dataset
- entries carry an adapter unless their kind is modifier or system
- familiar lifecycle rules (count only for group familiars) hold
- duplicate policy matches the kind (PROJECT_PLAN.md section 6.6)
- `attach_modifier` references resolve to modifier entries
- the gamble table covers rolls 1..20 exactly once
- power-score weights sum to 1.00 and respect their declared bounds
- ticket rolls satisfy min <= avg <= max; removal lists at least one item or tag
- `minecraft:` attributes name a 1.21.1 vanilla attribute
- advancement roots have a background and every parent resolves
- each loot table is injected by at most one band file
- every loot modifier file is listed in `global_loot_modifiers.json`

## Errors

None.

## Provenance

Schemas and content live in `mod/`; the default config is the answered D3
default; `chaoslab datapack --selftest` proves the validator
rejects malformed input; `chaoslab verify` regenerates this report offline
and pins it in `reports/MANIFEST.sha256`.
